### 3. The chart (the Chart Table displays; this system supplies the true values)

The Chart Table GDD owns the layout; these are the **visual rules its drawing of `web.view` must follow** (ART 7.4, 2.11, 3.4). The chart is a meta-register object: nothing on it is a reading, so nothing on it can glide or gleam.

| `web.view` fact | Drawing |
|---|---|
| HOME node | Tungsten fixture point (`#FFCC66`) with a 1-px rectilinear Foundry silhouette. The **only** warm mark besides a planned sortie and the unknown tick. |
| UNSEEN derelict (listed, selectable, OQ-WM-4) | Plain Graphite-lit rectangle. No dialect, no tick, no label beyond what selection shows. The single exception is a neutral ordinal ('1', '2', ...) in the Chart Table's compact list, assigned in roster order and carrying no identity, distance or cost (CT-44, ASK-CT-10). |
| SEEN or BOARDED derelict | Graphite-lit rectilinear silhouette in its donor dialect (MVP: Foundry only; worn). |
| `unknown_seen` | One **1-art-px square** Tungsten tick inside the silhouette, with a **1-px Ground clearing** around it so it survives 1x zoom and HR-12 distances. No circle (ART 3.4), no glow, no loop hue at MVP (both `hue_class` are Tungsten). Appears only once seen from the pod. |
| `berthed` derelict | Silhouette drawn **abutting** the home silhouette at the socket edge; the join is a solid 1-px **Hull Grey** seam (ART 4.2 integrity/history), not a Tungsten line. |
| ABSORBED | No longer a node; its silhouette is drawn **as part of the home silhouette** (the ship the crew built). No tick. |
| ORPHAN | Donor-dialect silhouette in **Graphite mid** (dim, known, dead), detached, **not selectable**, no tick. |
| `pod_at` HOME or a derelict id | A small rectilinear pod marker at that node (24x24 glyph class). |
| `pod_at` IN_FLIGHT | **No marker is interpolated.** No position, no progress, no ETA: the route is drawn as the planned sortie (Tungsten dashed) from `web.view.leg_target_id`, `leg_origin_id` and `leg_dir` only (OUT leg: HOME to the target; BACK leg: `leg_origin_id`, the departure derelict, to HOME; one segment, no direction mark), never from the pick (ASK-CT-3, CT-15) and the Chart Table may show a state word (`IN TRANSIT`) in the instrument face. `web.view` carries no progress datum (WM-36), so none can be drawn. *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)* |
| Selection | The ART 7.2 bracket (Tungsten bracket plus 1-px bezel thickening), gamepad snap node to node. A distance label beside it, if the Chart Table keeps one, must be the **chart's authored map distance**, never derived from `n`, velocity or ticks, and **no travel time and no fuel cost** may sit beside it (a pre-hop cost number is a Web Map & Transit meter; the debit is "a price you can see later", Player Fantasy beat 2). |
| Rings | Concentric Ground steps about 4 % darker toward the centre; all MVP nodes sit in ring 1; rings 2 and 3 are drawn as the horizon, not as content. Filaments Hull Grey, 1 art px, drift at most 1 art px per second in the current ring only. |

### 4. Palette rules

**Sanity palette.** Violet (hue 270 to 330) and Flesh (hue 5 to 30, S 20 to 60, V 45 to 85) appear **nowhere** in the exterior, the pod, the pool, the lamp, the filaments, the dock prop, the cradle or the chart. The only violet that can reach the screen during a hop is the stage-1 gleam on a *lying suit reading* in the HUD, which is Sanity's and is not altered by this system. The iridescence cycle is teal, green, bronze, gold with the 270 to 330 arc excluded (ART 4.1). **Near-case:** the iridescence "bronze" stop under a Tungsten pool is the web piece most likely to enter the flesh hue band; verify it stays hue 36 degrees or more, or saturation under 20 %, in-pool (ART 4.4).

**Loop hues.** None drawn in the exterior. Both MVP `visible_unknown` records are Tungsten; ART 7.4's "or its loop hue" option is unused at MVP (Vertical Slice may use a loop hue per `hue_class` and then ART 4.7 gets a row). The pod prop and cradle carry no loop hue.

**Luminance ladder (ART 4.5), applied to the leg, top to bottom:** lamp core (Tungsten, bright) > pool peak (Tungsten at reduced value; always below the lamp core) > lit Graphite hull inside the pool (L* about 43 or less) > Graphite mid housings > Void Steel hull tint (one to two steps above black, 8.11 flag 1) > **void `#000000`**. The lamp-versus-pool ordering is the contract; the hex values are tuned under the real renderer (ART 4.0).

**Ambient.** About 0 % in the leg at every ring (ART 2.5). The Void Steel hull tint is the only reason the wreck is visible beyond the pool; the contract is the **gap** (one to two steps over true black), measured by lighting-spike step 7.

**HR-15 (low-vision option).** The raised floor applies the same added steps to the **exterior hull tint** (hull geometry only). Filaments stay visible only inside the pool. The lamp predicate and halo are unchanged. No simulation effect.

**Chart palette (ART 4.6).** Ground `#1C2128` stepped darker toward the centre; Paper White text; Tungsten fixture for the home point, the planned sortie and the unknown tick; Hull Grey for filaments and the berth seam; Graphite lit / mid for wreck states. **No Alarm Red** on the chart or in the console prompt (HR-4: red is never text; a refusal is a rule, not an alarm). No violet, flesh, iridescence, Attention or sanity (ART 7.4).

### 5. Animation and motion constraints

| # | Constraint | Basis |
|---|---|---|
| M1 | Leg motion is **constant velocity**, whole-pixel steps per tick (BDY-26), pod-local camera, **no ease in, no ease out, no in-leg cut**. The zoom level survives the cuts; zoom input is honoured in the leg (a wider field shows more void and the same two lit things, nothing more). | PIPE C10, C2(iv); R7 |
| M2 | Pool, lamp core and halo are **steady**. No flicker (reads as deployable), no pulse (reads as power or alarm), no sweep (reads as alarm), no breathing. Fixed radius for the whole leg. | ART 4.2, 4.1 power/beam separation |
| M3 | **No second emission** in frame: no thruster, navigation strobe, exhaust glow, cabin light spill, or sparkle on filaments. | ART 2.5, Principle 3 |
| M4 | Filament **world scroll** is the leg velocity and is information, so it **never stops** under reduced motion. Filament **self-drift** (sway amplitude at most 2 art px at under 0.5 Hz) **does** stop under reduced motion (WM-34, HR-8). Filaments do not turn toward, lean toward or resolve more densely at the pool edge as the pod passes. | VA-R7, HR-8 |
| M5 | **No parallax** at MVP: exterior content sits on one plane against the pod-local camera. A second depth rate would imply a pose reading, and `[BIN]` `Parallax2D` behaviour under the integer-scale SubViewport is unverified. | PIPE C2; WM-37 |
| M6 | The lamp enters by scroll from the cull circle's edge, thickening through four ordered-dither steps over the fade band `EXTERIOR_CULL_FADE_PX` (no alpha fade, no scale pop, no one-frame appearance; at a field wider than the circle the lamp does not appear mid-frame at full strength) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. Hull enters the same way. The pod sprite has **one idle frame per facing**; it never animates (a drifting body has no moving part; "state in the sprite" is carried by the lamp's lit or dark state). | ART 3.5 (round and moving is reserved for heroes) |
| M7 | Luminance transitions in the whole sequence: two fades (at most 120 ms each, toward black) and two cuts, separated by at least `HOP_LEG_TICKS_MIN` 360 ticks and the settle window. No flash, no full-frame bright event. HR-13's 3 Hz ceiling is met by orders of magnitude. | HR-13, ART 7.3 |
| M8 | Contact Settle: `BERTH_SETTLE_PX` [PLACEHOLDER] 2 art px, 250 to 400 ms, overshoot at most 5 %, once. Release Settle: half of it. Both world-camera only; the HUD never moves. Removed under reduced motion; the sound and the prompt carry the event. (ART 7.3 defines Settle as an instrument profile, "the ship changed; the instrument caught up"; reusing the same curve as a world-camera impulse is this section's proposal, AB-8.) | ART 7.3 Settle; HR-6; HR-8 |
| M9 | Chart: cuts and 80 to 120 ms fades, 100 ms bracket ease-out, **no slides, no bounces, no parallax**; only the current ring's filaments drift (at most 1 art px per second); everything human is static. | ART 7.3 menus |
| M10 | During the hold and the leg the **suit readings are unchanged and honest in motion terms**: the O2 reserve falls at Suit's own rate while `IN_POD` (Suit `POD_RUN`, delegated ruling R6: 47,100 mGU over a nominal leg), so the O2 dial moves only as Suit moves it and must not breathe, tick or tremble from this system's state *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling R6)*. Any glide or gleam on a reading belongs to Sanity's stage 1 and is the HUD's. | ART 7.0 screen-space set, HR-3, WM-17 |
| M11 | The avatar and its carried module are not drawn in the leg (the pod is its own body); the carried item reappears with the avatar at the arrival cut. The black hold covers the discontinuity. | PCI:173 |

### 6. Art bible principles and sections that apply

| Section | Applies as |
|---|---|
| 1 Rule and Checklist | Q1 spend: the leg spends fuel (Graph); the lamp costs nothing to see but needs the pod's light. Q2 in the world: lamp lit or dark, hull, cradle, prop. Q3 leaks: nothing here can lie, so nothing leaks; if it ever did the palette rule is 4.4. |
| Principle 1 (darkness honest) | Void is black; hull is a tint, not a mask; the web is not drawn unlit; the leg never uses a vignette or fog. |
| Principle 3 (emission is exposure) | **Trade-off recorded for the creative director (ASK-AD-3):** the pod's pool looks like a lantern, which the bible says the web can see, while the sim treats it as cosmetic (WM-26, ASK-A1). Accepted at MVP because the leg is a truth surface with no stakes; made safe by VA-R7. If Attention ever makes the pod's light eligible, filaments gain a response and this section is revisited. |
| Corollary (lie leaks) | Not engaged in the exterior or on the chart. Anchors that must hold: HUD frame, true light radius (n/a in the leg), crewmate beam shape (chart glyph, Vertical Slice B). |
| 2.5 Pod transit | Governing mood: Smallness, ambient about 0 %, pod lights the sole source, filaments where light touches, residual human light as the unknown, **no nebula**. |
| 2.6 Boarding rhythm | The handoff: the arrival cut lands in the register of "forensic curiosity under a held breath"; no arrival fanfare. |
| 2.9 Low-sanity overlay | Scope amended per VA-R6 / AB-1. |
| 2.11 Menus and web map | Chart mood: charted, quiet, inward-pulling; concentric darkening. |
| 3.3 / 3.4 | Wreck hulls: Foundry grammar (worn atlas, DA E15). Chart: human geometry rectilinear over inhuman branching filaments; the unknown is a square, never a circle. |
| 3.5 hero order | In the leg the only hero is **#4, the derelict's one visible unknown**. There is no beam (#1) and no machinery (#2) in frame; the pool is a supporting source and must not out-contrast the lamp core. |
| 4.1 / 4.2 | Tungsten fixture `#FFCC66` for pool and lamp; Void Steel hull tint; Graphite lit on revealed hull; Hull Grey only on thin elements. |
| 4.3 | Leg ambient about 0 % regardless of ring; ring changes only iridescence intensity (MVP ring 1: low, sheen only within the pool). HOME ambient is independent of ring. |
| 4.4 | Asset test applies to the exterior: sample Hull Grey and lit Graphite under the pool; hue must stay 36 degrees or more; warm-lit Hull Grey is checked first (near-case in section 4). |
| 4.5 / 4.7 | The lamp core is readable under achromatopsia by luminance and by shape (hard square on a rectilinear hull); no loop hue is present in the exterior, so no 4.7 loop pair is active. |
| 6.1 | Wreck exterior keeps its donor dialect in full; worn Foundry atlas for wrecks, maintained for home. |
| 7.0 | The pod console prompt is meta register (interaction prompts); suit layer unchanged; no pod HUD mode. **Pod dash withdrawn at MVP (AB-2).** |
| 7.3 | Settle profile reused for the contact beat; Tremor and Glide are not used by this system. Reduced motion per HR-8. |
| 7.4 | The chart components table, verbatim (section 3). |
| 7.7 | HR-3 (suit set persists), HR-6 (menu/pause layers and HUD frame are anchors), HR-8, HR-13, HR-14 (gamepad snap), HR-15. |
| 8.2 / 8.5 / 8.7 | Prop 96x96 cell (AB-7); minimum feature 2x2; naming grammar; tier-1 silhouette (25 %) and tier-2 marks (50 %) gates on every asset in section 9. |
| 8.10 / 8.11 | Floor tint is the existing hull material; no sixth material; the pool and iridescence ride the existing per-segment iridescence material. |

### 7. Truth channel versus corruptible classification

**Every visual this system draws is a truth channel** (WM-35). Per item:

| Visual | Class | Reason / what may not touch it |
|---|---|---|
| `TRANSIT_HOLD` plate | T (non-diegetic, Pipeline) | Not a reading. |
| Pod sprite, running-light pool | T | A real body at a real place; the Filter may not move, hide or duplicate it. |
| Filaments in the leg | T | Function of ring and authored layout; **not** an Attention readout; the Filter may not add or remove. |
| Wreck silhouette and hull | T | Static data. |
| Residual-lamp point and halo | T | Chain predicate over persisted Graph state (WM-18, OR-4). **Forbidden list (alongside DA:1188):** a second lit point, an erased or dimmed or moved lamp, a lamp lit by the Filter when the predicate is false, a flicker. |
| Dark lamp housing | T | Same predicate, false branch. |
| Docked-podcraft prop and empty cradle | T | Renders iff `pod_at` equals that dock. **Forbidden for the Filter:** a phantom dock prop (a pod that is not there) or an erased one (a pod that is). Both are lies about a real body and would be a lethal lie if the player trusts the hop (ASK-F1). |
| Contact and release Settle | T | Event-driven from `BERTHED` / `UNBERTHED`. |
| Pod console **prompt and refusal reasons** | **M, meta register** | Drawn on the chart-table-register Ground plate (ART 7.0 "interaction prompts"), **not** in the thin moving **reading register** of ART 3.4. The reading register carries the lie-capable declaration (and the stage-1 gleam); refusal reasons are truth-only (ASK-F1, CD-4), so they must never be drawn as an instrument reading. No glide, no gleam, no tick animation. |
| Chart (all of `web.view`) | M | Not an instrument (ART 7.4). |
| FUEL_TANK reserve band (Graph / Authoring art; ASK-G3, ASK-AD-9; not drawn by this system) | T | Two static painted facts at the refusal thresholds (9.5 % and 11.5 % of the tank at the defaults), derived from `HOP_FUEL_FLOOR_ML` [PLACEHOLDER] plus the verb costs *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. Never wrapped, moved, erased, relocated or animated. They mark where the pod will and will not leave and say nothing about the current level (that is the sight-glass, L). |
| **Not this system's, lie-capable, on screen during the hop** | L | The suit HUD readings (the frame is an anchor) and the home fuel sight-glass (the Graph's corruptible module-body channel). Their consequence witnesses (puddle, drip, SCRAM) are the Graph's truth. This system adds nothing to either. |

**Hallucination classes versus this system's visuals.** (a) Phantom entities: none in the leg or on the chart; a phantom may appear in a dock room after arrival, which is the derelict's space and the Filter's business. (b) False system states: not the console reasons, `pod_at`, the prop, the lamp. (c) Crewmates as horrors (Vertical Slice B): the pod has one seat; the chart's crewmate glyph is the beam-shape identity, never faked. (d) Phantom salvage: this system draws no loot; the carried item travels unchanged. Stage 2 (light lie) has no cone to extend in the leg.

### 8. Audio: visual-mood descriptors only

The audio-director owns every sound, layer, fade and trigger; this system emits events (WM-31) and owns no sound.

- **Leg:** suspended, silent, vast, threaded, watched (ART 2.5). Energy low; the one spike is the wreck resolving. Nothing in the picture asks for a stinger.
- **Resolve:** a slightly wrong warmth: somebody else's lantern.
- **Dock arrival:** the held breath of ART 2.6, a hush that arrives with the cut, not before it.
- **Berth approach (BERTHING):** a sustained drive-hum and hull-strain bed for the whole of the closing (`BERTH_TICKS` [PLACEHOLDER]): heavy, patient, non-positional (no direction, no Doppler, no sense of the ship's own motion, `interior_invariance`), ending into the contact cue. It is the berth's duration witness, so a berth is heard to take time and to burn something. It must not read as the Tell (no rising irregular rumble). *Rejected alternatives, recorded:* a dust or loose-item settle on the contact beat (a new animated element, against "no new element appears" under reduced motion and the spirit of BDY-28) and an airlock external-indicator state change (a new fixture state needing an Authoring and Lighting ask, and liable to read as a gauge).
- **Hull contact (berth):** a settled, heavy, soft meeting, not an impact; the visual Settle begins no later than the audio cue. A dock must sound and look nothing like the Tell (no tremor-like shudder, no rising irregular rumble).
- `LEG_BED_FADE_TICKS` [PLACEHOLDER] 60 is the audio-director's and is left as authored. Designed (`adaptive-audio-and-tells.md`): adopted by Audio AA-33 / D.17.
- *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* **Leg hush.** The MVP leg bed's assets are Vertical Slice (Audio AA-7), so an unaltered MVP leg is silent, which confounds AC-WM-81b ("a loading screen"). A placeholder leg hush ships at MVP: one quiet looped bed started by `POD_DEPARTED` and ended by `POD_ARRIVED`, an Audio-owned asset (OQ-WM-46).
- **The Tell bed stays audible during LEG.** The protected bed continues (dread persists; the relief arrives at the arrival cut, not at departure), so the hop must not sound like relief; a rule for Audio (AA-33, E-AA-55; WM-26b). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **Berth bed level.** A LOUD act (WM-26) heard only as a -44 dBr tier-4 bed that ducks under every tier-2 cue and has no minimum-audibility margin reads wrong, and it is the berth's only audio witness. Audio revisits the level, adds a minimum-audibility margin and a berth-apply onset cue, and fixes AA-7's table, which lists the berth bed as both MVP and cut-first (OQ-WM-46). The L-10 tag lint, not a spectral test, is the MVP check that the bed is not the Tell (AC-WM-117). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **Departure-body emitter cull and first-frame audio at settle (owners named).** At the departure apply the departed derelict's emitters stop being live (a dormant section's lamp is not live, EM:660) and its light must not draw in the leg: owner Emitter State and the PROJECTOR, criterion AC-WM-131. At the `pod_settle` apply the listener context switches to the target body in that same apply, so the first frame after the cut has the target's room tone and no leg hush: owner Audio (AA-33, E-AA-55), criterion AC-WM-132. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*

### 9. Asset types implied

All names follow ART 8.5 `<cat>[_<dialect>]_<subject>[_<variant tokens>]_<map>.png`, lowercase, two-digit indices, Aseprite JSON sidecars, at least 4 px transparent padding per non-tile cell (8.6). Cells are base px. **Gate tiers (ART 8.7):** tier 1 silhouette at 25 % and tier 2 marks at 50 % on every row.

| # | Asset | Stem(s) | Cell / notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| A1 | Pod exterior | `prop_shared_podcraft_ext_alb.png` (+ `_nrm`, `_msk` for the running-light emissive); Aseprite tags `idle_s`, `idle_se`, `idle_e`, `idle_ne`, `idle_n`; others mirrored in engine (mirrors are never files) | 96x96 cell, hull envelope about 80x56 art px, rectilinear Foundry; one running-light housing cluster on the centreline; Graphite lit body, Hull Grey thin trim only; minimum feature 2x2 survives 50 %. **Vehicle token:** ART 8.5 has no `cat` for a vehicle; this section reuses `prop` with the new variant token `ext` (exterior view) rather than add a category for one object (AB-3). |
| A2 | Running-light pool mask | `light_pool_pod.png` | Greyscale radial falloff texture (ART 8.10 light-texture row); **used as a mask and additive tint, not on a `Light2D`** (R6). Radius `LEG_RUNNING_LIGHT_RADIUS_PX` [PLACEHOLDER] 96 base px is a **ceiling**; the 10 %-of-peak contour should sit at about 0.7 of it. |

## Game Feel

*Feel is specified as numbers at 60 ticks per second (one tick is 16.7 ms; the Pipeline renders at the display rate and interpolates with `tick_alpha`). The sequence below uses the default [PLACEHOLDER] values (`HOP_LEG_TICKS` [PLACEHOLDER] 900, `ARRIVE_SETTLE_TICKS` [PLACEHOLDER] 45, `BERTH_TICKS` [PLACEHOLDER] 624); a retune changes the numbers and not the shape. Feel here is mostly the **absence** of the usual game-feel levers: the crossing is a committed, hands-off, quiet act, and every missing lever (hit-stop, shake, rumble, slow-motion, a reward sting) is missing on purpose (anti-pillars; Player Fantasy guard rails 1 to 8, restated in the Weight profile below).*

### Feel Reference

- **Reference.** The *commitment* of an FTL jump (one decision, one debit, no cancel) crossed with the *dark* of Sunless Sea (the default state of the sea is unlit; a small lit ship is a target) and the *known way home* of Project Zomboid (every trip out has a way back, and the world you left is the world you return to). The quality borrowed is **spent, small and cold**: the player presses once and is carried; nothing they do on the crossing changes it; the only thing that changes is what they see at the far end.
- **Anti-reference.** Not Outer Wilds (free flight is the core verb; here the player never steers, throttles or lands, and PCI:217 forbids it). Not a loading screen with a spinner (the black hold has no indicator of any kind). Not a cinematic docking sequence (no QTE, no braking flare). Not a fast-travel menu (the pick is a chart act, the press is the commitment, and the crossing takes its fifteen seconds every time).
- **Tone words** (from the creative direction): *committed, small, honest, cold, spent.* Words the feel must never read as: *journey, voyage, warp, jump drive, unlock, discover.*

### Input Responsiveness

The only player input this system owns is the **console press** (`pod_hop`, and on the home console `berth` and `unberth`). It is a single press of Interaction's `interact` on the in-reach console (lit is required for all three verbs, IA-6; the `pod_hop` exemption ASK-I2 stays refused and the console is lit by the pod's own status glow, WM-8b, OQ-WM-39) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*, a non-channelled (instant) verb with no hold-to-confirm and no confirmation dialog (the Chart Table pick was the deliberate step; the press is the point of no return, guard rail 1) *(provisional — Interaction's declaration row for `pod_hop` is "Web Map's", IA:131; ASK-I1)*. During LEG, ARRIVING and BERTHING-as-a-hop there is **no input to be responsive to** (locomotion intents are applied and ignored, PCI:173); that is a design choice, not a latency target.

| Action | Max input-to-response latency | Frame budget (60 fps) | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| Console press while eligible: command validated and applied | 1 tick of simulation (16.7 ms) plus at most one render frame of input sampling = 33 ms | at most 2 frames | The apply is in the kernel's P2 of the tick after the sampled input; the departure is one atomic apply (WM-10), never split across ticks. |
| Console press: hold plate begins to fade in | 120 ms fade starting the same frame the apply is observed (PIPE:264) | 7 frames of fade | The fade is the Pipeline's; this system adds no delay. |
| Console press refused: reason shown | at most 33 ms | at most 2 frames | The first failing reason of WM-9, as words on Interaction's chart-table-register plate; no state changes, no sound that reads as an alarm. A refusal must read as a *rule*, instantly, never as a fault. |
| Console prompt appears when the console becomes eligible (lit, in reach) | Interaction's prompt timing, not restated here | n/a | The prompt is Interaction's; this system supplies the pure `console_ready` predicate. |
| `berth` / `unberth` press | as the `pod_hop` press | at most 2 frames | No fade (the avatar stays aboard); the machine enters BERTHING at the apply tick. |
| Pause during LEG (solo) | immediate (the tick stops; nothing advances, no slip) | 0 | The leg is not skippable, but it is pausable (E-11). |
| Zoom input during LEG | the Pipeline's ladder response | n/a | Honoured; a wider field shows more void and the same two lit things. |

### Animation Feel Targets

Startup is the wind-up before the action has effect; active is the action happening; recovery is the committed frames after it. All values are ticks at 60 per second; there is **no wind-up anywhere** (a drifting body has no moving part).

| Animation | Startup | Active | Recovery | Feel goal | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Hop departure (press to first leg frame) | 0 ticks wind-up; 7 ticks (120 ms) fade to black, then the Pipeline's cut to the pod body | 900 ticks leg (15 s) | `ARRIVE_SETTLE_TICKS` [PLACEHOLDER] 45 ticks (750 ms) of black | Committed, then suspended. No ease in or out: the pod is at constant velocity from the first integration to the last. | Total time in the pod 945 ticks (15.75 s). Fades are 120 ms each, toward black only. |
| Pod motion in the leg | 0 | `v` px per tick on each axis, whole pixels (default `v = (1, 0)`, 60 base px per second) | pod stops in one tick at `p1` (velocity 0) at `pod_arrive` | Slow, small, weightless: the world goes by a fixed pod. | No in-leg cut, no ease, no rotation, no idle animation (one frame per facing). |
| Residual lamp resolving | 0 | enters at the field edge and is in view for `view_ticks` at least `LAMP_VIEW_MIN_TICKS` [PLACEHOLDER] 90 (112 ticks, 1.9 s, at the narrowest field at `v = (1, 0)`) | stays in view to the last frame | Seen, not missed: one warm point that arrives by scroll, never flares. | The reveal window is lint-enforced (D.6, AC-WM-68). |
| Arrival cut (`pod_settle`) | 0 | 0 (a hard cut, the first frame is final) | the held breath of boarding begins at once | Abrupt and quiet: from black to a dark room with a pool of chest light. | No fade, no fanfare, no text (guard rail 5). |
| Berth approach (BERTHING) | 0 | `BERTH_TICKS` [PLACEHOLDER] 624 ticks (10.4 s) in two phases, last 24 ticks at 1 px per tick | 0 | Patient and heavy: a slow closing, felt as sound and a still world. | The avatar acts normally throughout; no exterior is shown. The sustained drive-hum and hull-strain bed is the duration witness (the closing is heard to take 10.4 s); it is not keyed to a progress datum (there is none, WM-36). |
| Contact Settle (`BERTHED`) | 0 | `BERTH_SETTLE_PX` [PLACEHOLDER] 2 art px impulse, damped spring 15 to 24 ticks (250 to 400 ms), overshoot at most 5 % | 0 (still) | A soft, settled meeting; never an impact, never a tremor. | World camera only; HUD still; removed under reduced motion. *(provisional — ASK-AD-6)* |
| Release Settle (`unberth`) | 0 | 1 art px, same profile | 0 | Half as strong as the contact. | No end-of-path beat. |

### Impact Moments

This system has **one** impact moment, and it is deliberately small.

| Impact type | Duration | Effect description | Configurable? |
|---|---|---|---|
| Hit-stop (freeze frames) | none | None. Death and harm are not caused by this system; a hop removes the player from danger and starts none. | no |
| Screen shake | none | None, anywhere in the hop. The Tremor is the Tell's signature (ART 2.7) and must never appear in a crossing or a dock (BDY-28: ship motion applies no force, tilt or knockback to any actor). | no |
| Camera impact | 250 to 400 ms | The berth contact Settle: `BERTH_SETTLE_PX` [PLACEHOLDER] 2 art px (at most `JOLT_PX`'s registered maximum of 2) along the socket axis, damped, once (release half). The weld's own contact keeps Assembly's `JOLT_PX` 0 (MSA VA-19); this is a separate profile on this system's `BERTHED` event. | yes, `BERTH_SETTLE_PX` [PLACEHOLDER] (0 to 2); forced to 0 by reduced motion |
| Controller rumble | none | None from this system. A crossing does not buzz the pad. | no |
| Time-scale slowdown | none | None. The leg runs at real speed; it is not skippable (WM-34) and not slowed. | no |
| Fade (the boundary beats) | 120 ms in, 120 ms toward the hold | The two black fades that bracket the leg (PIPE:264). Not an impact; listed so no one adds a flash. | the Pipeline's |
| Sting / banner / "arrived" text | none | None. Arrival is a cut to the dock room (guard rail 5); no reward is attached to a crossing. | no |

### Weight and Responsiveness Profile

- **Weight.** Heavy and deliberate at the edges, weightless in the middle. The press has the weight of a door that locks behind you; the leg has no weight at all (a small lit hull drifting in a vast dark, ART 2.5 "Smallness"); the berth is the heaviest thing here, a slow closing of two hulls.
- **Player control.** High before the press (the player chooses a target at the chart table, can walk away, can check the tank and the lamp), **none after it**. The player cannot cancel, steer, skip or speed the crossing. That loss of control is the designed feeling: the commitment is felt, and the only recourse for a bad call is the way back at no fuel. The pause button is available but changes nothing.
- **Snap quality.** Crisp and binary at the boundaries (a press, a fade to black, a cut to the pod, a fade to black, a cut to the dock), smooth and analog in between (constant velocity). There are exactly two fades and two cuts in a hop, separated by at least 360 ticks plus the settle window.
- **Acceleration model.** None. The pod is at its authored constant velocity from the first tick and stops in one tick; no ramp, no ease. The simulation is exact (the pod is at `p1` at arrival), which the feel rewards with a perfectly steady drift.
- **Failure texture.** Fair and legible. A refused press names the rule in words (`FUEL_LOW`, `BUSY`, `BERTH_PENDING`, and so on) on a plate that is not an alarm and not an instrument reading, so the player always knows *why* nothing happened and never suspects a bug. A hop is never an unwarned harm: nothing random happens on the crossing, the leg takes the same time every time, and the derelict at the far end is exactly as it was left (the web's distances do not change; a hop never refills a room). The fuel cost is a price paid later: the player feels it as the reactor timer and the sight-glass, not as a toll displayed on the pick.
- **Berth weight.** The berth has no picture, so its weight is carried by sound and contact: a drive bed heard for the whole closing, then a soft meeting. Without the bed the crossing would be asserted, not witnessed. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* **Non-audio witness (open gap):** the bed and the contact Settle (removed under reduced motion) are the only witnesses, which is an accessibility gap for a player who cannot hear. A console-register state word 'BERTHING' was proposed and is not adopted here because it contradicts Chart Table CT-15 and EC-CT-25 (designed: 'BERTHING has no word') if placed on the chart; it is recorded as OQ-WM-45 for Interaction's console plate.
- **Under hallucination.** The crossing is the one stretch where the world layer cannot lie. Nothing about its feel changes with sanity: no extra drift, no phantom light, no changed timing. The player learns to lean on it as a perceptual reset of the world (not of the mind).

### Feel Acceptance Criteria

Specific, testable by a playtester without instruments; each maps to a numbered acceptance criterion.

- [ ] At least two of three outside testers mention the residual lamp or the exterior unprompted (AC-WM-81a).
- [ ] No tester describes the hop as a loading screen, and none asks for a skip before the fourth hop (AC-WM-81b, 81c).
- [ ] Testers describe the warm point as something they saw, not missed (AC-WM-82a), and the in-pod time over a scripted four-leg session is at most 6 % of the session (AC-WM-82b). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*
- [ ] The press-to-fade latency is imperceptible: the plate begins to fade within two frames of the press (this system's half is AC-WM-138, auto; the fade itself is the Pipeline's criterion; observed in AC-WM-29; AC-WM-77 measures the hook's cost, a different quantity). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*
- [ ] Nobody reports a stutter, jolt or lurch during a leg at any zoom step; the pod's motion reads as perfectly steady (AC-WM-80, AC-WM-79; the vocabulary check is AC-WM-81i). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*
- [ ] Testers describe the berth as a closing that took time and cost something, heard and not seen, and none reports it as a teleport (AC-WM-117 wiring, AC-WM-117b quality, AC-WM-81j). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*
- [ ] No tester reports berthing or hopping as a way to dodge the Web's attention for free (AC-WM-81g).
- [ ] No reviewer uses the words *shaky*, *loading*, *floaty-fast* or *teleport* for the crossing or the berth; the berth contact reads as a soft meeting and not as the Tell (AC-WM-115, AC-WM-81i). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*
- [ ] No tester reports the pod refusing them during a tell or breach (AC-WM-81e, telemetry-backed with a scripted breach; the safety claim itself is the automated AC-WM-61 and the lit-gate ACs), and no tester reports `FUEL_LOW` as arbitrary or hidden (AC-WM-81h) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.
- [ ] No tester reports a derelict refilling or a lamp relit between visits (AC-WM-81f).
- [ ] Reduced motion keeps the crossing fully legible: scroll continues, drift stops, no flash (AC-WM-30, AC-WM-81k). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*

## UI Requirements

**This system has no UI of its own.** It owns no HUD element, no menu, no screen, no widget and no instrument (guard rail 1: a sentence that describes a Web Map & Transit HUD element is a defect). It publishes **true data** (`web.view`, the `console_ready` predicate, the closed refusal-reason set, the events of WM-31) and **constrains** the surfaces that display them; the surfaces themselves belong to the neighbours named below. No UX Flag is raised against this GDD, because no `/ux-design` screen is authored for it: the surfaces it feeds will be specified in `design/ux/hud.md` (not yet written; the Interaction prompt plate), the Chart Table's own spec (the pick, the chart, the pause panel) and the Pipeline's `TRANSIT_HOLD` row. When those specs are written they must cite this section for the constraints in the right-hand column of the table below.

### Information the player needs, and who displays it

| Information | Display location (owner) | Update frequency | Condition | Constraint this GDD places on the surface |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| The derelicts that exist, their `status` and `unknown_seen`, `pod_at`, `transit_state` | The chart at the chart table (Chart Table, designed; reads `web.view`) | on open and on `web.view` change (event-driven; a chart open is host-local UI, never SIM) | the chart is open (single-player pauses; co-op does not, KRN CR-16) | Drawing rules of Visual/Audio section 3: UNSEEN derelicts are listed and selectable as bare nodes; nothing is revealed beyond `status` and `unknown_seen`; no interpolated pod position, no ETA, no cost, no danger rating, no "recommended" marker. (Chart Table designed, `chart-table-web-map-and-menus.md`) |
| The pick (which derelict the crew means to visit) | The chart (Chart Table) | on selection | the chart is open | Host-local presentation state; never SIM, never persisted, never a field of `web.state` (WM-8, AC-WM-88). The console press is the SIM write. |
| That a pod console exists and is usable | Interaction's prompt plate at the console's screen position (Interaction, ART 7.0) | while the console is the focused subject | `console_ready`: `pod_at` equals this dock, machine IDLE, console in reach, and lit (required for all three verbs; the `pod_hop` exemption stays refused and the console is lit by the pod's own status glow, WM-8b, OQ-WM-39) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* | Prompt only; no Web Map & Transit element. The console is the only trigger for `pod_hop` / `berth` / `unberth`. The plate sits on the meta-register Ground, not the reading register. |
| Why a press was refused | The same Interaction plate, as words (reason set of WM-9) | at the refused press | the press was refused | Truth-only (ASK-F1); shown as words, no number, no Alarm Red (HR-4), never as an instrument reading; the first failing reason in WM-9's order. A refusal that depends on fuel (`FUEL_LOW`) names the rule and never the quantity. |
| That the pod is where it is | The docked-podcraft prop, or the empty cradle, in the dock room (Layout / Authoring art; WM-33) | on dock-room entry and on `pod_at` change | always, in a loaded dock room | Renders iff `pod_at` equals that dock; never an overlay or a marker. |
| That a crossing is in progress | `TRANSIT_HOLD` plate and the exterior leg (Pipeline; this system's presentation content) | the 945-tick sequence | `transit_state` is LEG or ARRIVING | No spinner, ring, percentage, ETA, tip or text on the plate or in the leg (guard rail 1). The HUD stays up unchanged and lie-capable. |
| That fuel was spent | The Graph's own witnesses: the home tank sight-glass, the drip, the puddle (`PUDDLE` id 25), SCRAM (Graph / HUD instruments) | per the Graph | always | **No Web Map & Transit fuel meter, gauge or cost label** anywhere, including beside the pick (AB-5). The debit is "a price you can see later". |
| That a fuel reserve floor exists, and where | Two painted reserve marks (pod 9.5 %, berth 11.5 % at the defaults) on the home FUEL_TANK body (Graph / Authoring art; ASK-G3 accepted as prop art, ASK-AD-9 open) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* | static; never changes at runtime | the home tank is in view | A world mark, not a gauge: no number, no animation, no Web Map & Transit overlay, never wrapped by the Filter. They mark the refusal thresholds (below the pod mark the pod will not leave), not the current level (the Graph's witnesses carry that); `FUEL_LOW` still names the rule in words when the press is refused. |
| That a berth is in progress or complete | The world itself (a still ship), the sustained drive-hum and hull-strain bed across BERTHING, the contact Settle and cue, and Assembly's weld prompt once `docked` holds | at the berth's phase changes | `transit_state` is BERTHING; `docked` true | No exterior shown through ports, no progress element; a console-register state word was proposed and is not adopted (OQ-WM-45), so the bed and the Settle are the only witnesses, an open accessibility gap *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. |
| That a derelict was boarded, welded or cut off | The chart (Chart Table) per Visual/Audio section 3: `berthed` abutting seam, ABSORBED part of the home silhouette, ORPHAN dim and not selectable | on `web.view` change | the chart is open | Consequences appear on the chart only; a derelict never regrows or relights between visits. |
| `SaveInfo.depth_label` (`RING <n>`) | The Chart Table main menu (Continue line) only; the pause panel shows one recency fact (SL UI-2) (Chart Table, Save/Load; ASK-CT-4, C13) | at save capture | always | A label with no progress semantics; no percentage of the web charted, no completion flag (guard rail 5). |

### What no surface may carry (structural, not a promise)

- `web.view` has no field for fuel, Attention, sanity or any progress datum (WM-36, AC-WM-60), so no surface can display one by reading it. The Audio-only read `web.leg_timing()` is not part of `web.view` and presenting it as an ETA or progress element is a defect.
- The refusal reasons, `pod_at`, the lamp point, the prop and the chart are truth channels: the Perception Filter and Sanity may not wrap them (ASK-F1, ASK-AD-8).
- No authored text (no "Derelict A reached", no log drop, no caption) appears on any surface this system feeds (anti-pillar: no authored story).

### Input and accessibility notes for the surfaces

- **Gamepad.** The console press uses Interaction's `interact` binding on the focus the Controller already provides; the chart's gamepad snap is node to node (HR-14), Chart Table's. No new binding is introduced by this system.
- **Reduced motion.** Governs filament drift and the contact Settle only (Visual/Audio section 5); the pod's scroll is information and stays. No flashing element exists anywhere in a hop (HR-8, HR-13).
- **Colour independence.** No state in this system is carried by colour alone: the lamp is a hard square on a rectilinear hull (readable by luminance and shape, ART 4.5), refusals are words, the chart's node states differ by silhouette and mark (ART 4.7).
- **Text.** Refusal words and the chart's state word (`IN TRANSIT`) use the HUD's UI-scale and cap-height rules (the Pipeline's HUD-scale labels); this GDD fixes no size.
- **Localization.** Every refusal reason has a string key owned by Interaction's reason table (ASK-I1 accepted); no sentence is composed from fragments. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*

## Acceptance Criteria

*Tags: (auto) automated test, gdUnit4 headless, `tests/unit/web_map_transit/` or `tests/integration/web_map_transit/`; (lint) content, schema or CI lint; (review) design or code review; (manual) observed run or playtest with a retained artifact; (spike) engine-risk spike with a recorded result; (perf) a performance measurement in `tests/performance/web_map_transit/` on RM-DESKTOP and RM-DECK with the machine and build recorded (ADVISORY; pending spike measurement) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. Ids `AC-WM-nn`. Each criterion cites the rule (`WM-n`), formula (`D.n`) or edge case (`E-nn`) that it proves. Numbers are the [PLACEHOLDER] values of the Tuning Knobs section: a retune changes the numbers, never the criterion. A criterion that depends on a decision or ask that is still open (ASK-G1b, the ASK-PIPE draw rule, ASK-L1, ASK-MSA-1) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)* carries *(provisional — X)* and is recorded **NOT ASSESSED**, not PASS, until it is answered; criteria that depended only on asks the Designed neighbours have accepted are no longer provisional and are BLOCKING *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. One test slug is used throughout: `web_map_transit` (`tests/unit/web_map_transit/`, `tests/integration/web_map_transit/`, `tests/performance/web_map_transit/`). Visual and UI criteria retain a screenshot in `production/qa/evidence/` before the story closes (coding standard: a parse check is not a run). Tests are deterministic: no random source, no wall-clock assertion, every fixture a fixed table or a factory. This section was reviewed by the qa-lead (stage 6); its rewrites and additions are merged below, and the five defects it found in the GDD's own arithmetic are resolved in the criteria they touch (F-1 in AC-WM-15a/15b, AC-WM-54 and AC-WM-76 and in Edge Case E-23; F-4 in AC-WM-82; F-5 in AC-WM-37; F-6 in AC-WM-48; F-7 in AC-WM-08).*

### Group 1. Block, hook, records and `init_world`

- **AC-WM-01 (auto)** GIVEN the boot registry resolves WHEN `web.state` and `transit.step` register THEN the block has `persist` true, `persist_scope` WORLD, `scope` SHARED, `repl_hint` SHARED_SUMMARY, `schema_version` 1, `supported_versions` [1] and `restore_after` exactly `[layout.state, graph.state, body.state]` (no `controller.state`), and the hook has `divisor` 1, `offset` 0 and `after` exactly `[body.integrate, graph.propagate.failure]`; an unknown `after` id fails boot; and the command types register as WM-9 declares: `pod_hop`, `berth`, `unberth` with `allowed_origins {PLAYER}`, `actor_binding OWNED_BY_ISSUER`, actor_source `body.state`, coalesce `NONE`, max_age_ticks 120 (the same registration with actor_source omitted fails boot `ACTOR_SOURCE_INVALID` naming the type), and `pod_arrive`, `pod_settle`, `berth_step`, `web_reconcile` with `allowed_origins {SYSTEM}`, `actor_binding NONE` and no actor_source (declaring one fails `ACTOR_SOURCE_INVALID`); a `pod_hop` whose actor is not in the issuer's `body.state` binding set is rejected `ACTOR_NOT_OWNED` at P2 and a PLAYER `pod_arrive` is `ORIGIN_NOT_ALLOWED`. [WM-2, WM-9, CR-3, CR-6, CR-7, CR-8] *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix, G6)*
- **AC-WM-02 (auto)** GIVEN two runs of the same command stream from the same `mvp_world.json` WHEN `state_hash` is computed every tick THEN the two hash sequences are identical, and the block's hash row lists records in `mvp_world.json` order with a fixed field order and no dictionary iteration. [WM-2, WM-37, KRN D.12]
- **AC-WM-03 (lint)** GIVEN the record schema WHEN linted THEN every field is an integer, enum or byte string (no `Vector2i`, no string, no narrative field, no "reached the centre" flag, no completion flag), and `loaded` and `berthed` are not persisted fields. [WM-3, OR-2, guard rail 5]
- **AC-WM-04 (auto)** GIVEN `init_world(world_seed)` WHEN it completes THEN exactly four bodies exist (home, A, B, pod), HOME is BOARDED with `first_boarded_T` 0, A and B are UNSEEN, `pod_at` is HOME, `transit_state` is IDLE, the avatar stands on `spawn_cryo_1`, the A, B and pod sections are dormant, and the call order matches lint `INIT_WORLD_ORDER`. [WM-7, WM-24]
- **AC-WM-05 (auto)** GIVEN a rejected call injected at each step of `init_world` in turn WHEN the build runs THEN the whole build fails with `SL_NEW_GAME_NO_BASELINE` and no partial world is left. [WM-7, E-68]
- **AC-WM-06 (auto, Integration)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* GIVEN a breadth-first state-space exploration whose **de-duplication key** is `K` = (the canonical hash of `web.state`, Layout's body table, and the Graph's `dormant` flag per section), with the commands {hop A, hop B, hop HOME, berth, unberth, weld, cut}, each command run to quiescence (machine `IDLE`), the search ending when no unseen key remains (more than 10,000 distinct keys fails the test as non-convergent) WHEN the built-body count, the `build_section` call count and the live-body count (Lighting's definition: built and with a non-dormant section or hosting the avatar, LO-24) are sampled after every apply THEN built bodies never exceed 4, the `build_section` call count stays 4, and **live bodies never exceed 2, against `BODIES_LIVE_MAX` [PLACEHOLDER] 4**. A literal enumeration of seven commands to depth eight is about 5.8 million sequences of up to roughly 1,000 ticks each and is not used; the de-duplicated search converges in a few dozen keys. [WM-5, WM-24, lint `BODY_COUNT_MVP`, CF-LY]
- **AC-WM-07 (auto)** GIVEN the pod body WHEN `room_at` is queried on any pod cell THEN it answers `pod_cabin`, never NONE, and AC-BDY-29a still passes on a clean build. [WM-5, BDY-29]
- **AC-WM-08 (lint)** GIVEN `mvp_world.json` WHEN linted THEN `world_slot` values are distinct and in 0..7; each `init_world` pose equals `(slot x SLOT_PITCH_PX [PLACEHOLDER], 0)` (defaults: poses (0, 0), (16384, 0), (32768, 0), (49152, 0)); `slot_max x SLOT_PITCH_PX [PLACEHOLDER] <= WEB_COORD_BUDGET_PX [PLACEHOLDER]`; `SLOT_PITCH_PX [PLACEHOLDER]^2 >= (2 x EXTERIOR_VISIBLE_RADIUS_PX [PLACEHOLDER] + MAX_SECTION_EXTENT_PX [PLACEHOLDER])^2`; and `WEB_COORD_BUDGET_PX [PLACEHOLDER] <= WORLD_COORD_LIMIT [PLACEHOLDER] / 32`. Adjacent slots are exactly `SLOT_PITCH_PX` apart by construction, so "apart" is the D.14 inequality, never a strict "within" test. [WM-6, D.14, E-65]
- **AC-WM-08b (auto)** GIVEN a recorded full route WHEN every re-origin is checked THEN each moves all live-content bodies by one identical vector (pairwise relative offsets unchanged; lint `REORIGIN_IS_TRANSLATION`), and every dormant body outside the leg is re-posed to `init_pose(slot) - init_pose(departure slot)` (WM-6), with no two bodies within `SLOT_PITCH_PX` of each other *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. [WM-6, D.14, E-65]
- **AC-WM-09 (lint)** GIVEN the content tree WHEN grepped THEN the HOME payer tank is never found by the string `ft_main`, and `legs.json` is the only place leg numbers live (data-driven). [WM-20, WM-11]

### Group 2. The hop (command, departure, leg, arrival)

- **AC-WM-10 (auto)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* GIVEN every refusal case, each tested with every LATER condition that can co-occur with it also failing, to prove the order (the precedence table below lists the pairs that cannot co-occur; each infeasible pair is tested pairwise instead: the earlier reason alone is asserted when only it holds, and the later one when only it holds), WHEN `pod_hop` is pressed THEN the first failing reason in the order `BUSY`, `IN_TRANSIT`, `OUT_OF_RANGE`, `NOT_LIT`, `NOT_DOCKED_HERE`, `BERTH_PENDING` (payload-less form), `TARGET_UNKNOWN`, `TARGET_ABSORBED`, `TARGET_HERE`, `TARGET_BERTHED`, `BERTH_PENDING` (payload form), `FUEL_LOW`, `ARRIVAL_BLOCKED`, `CAPACITY` is returned, no state changes, no fuel moves, and `POD_NOT_READY` is never returned at MVP; `NOT_LIT` is returned for `pod_hop`, `berth` and `unberth` alike when the console is unlit (IA-6; ASK-I2 refused), exercised through a test double that disables the pod glow, since WM-8b makes it unreachable at a pod console; `CAPACITY` is exercised through a capacity test double, since no production state reaches it. *(User ruling 2026-10-27, OQ-WM-39: `NOT_LIT` stays in the reason set but is unreachable at a pod console and AC-WM-128 governs; no longer provisional.)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* [WM-9, E-16, E-45, E-46]
- **AC-WM-11 (auto)** GIVEN a PLAYER-origin `pod_arrive`, `pod_settle`, `berth_step` or `web_reconcile` WHEN submitted THEN the result is `ORIGIN_NOT_ALLOWED` and the machine is unchanged. [WM-9, E-10]
- **AC-WM-12 (auto)** GIVEN the pod at HOME with the pool at 20,000 mL WHEN `pod_hop{A}` applies at tick 10000 THEN in one apply 400 mL is withdrawn (pool 19,600), the avatar is on the pod body at `seat_pod_1`, BM7 poses and BM6 velocity are set, `transit_state` is LEG, `leg_epoch` increases by 1, `t0` is 10000, `pod_at` is IN_FLIGHT, and `POD_DEPARTED` is delivered in P5 Notify (not P4). [WM-10, WM-31, D.4]
- **AC-WM-13 (auto)** GIVEN the same hop WHEN run uninterrupted with `n` 900 THEN the hook submits `pod_arrive{leg_epoch}` at tick 10899, it applies at 10900, `integrations` equals 900 and the pod pose equals `p1` exactly. [WM-11, D.1, D.2]
- **AC-WM-14 (auto)** GIVEN a fixed table of 64 legs (the content legs plus boundary legs: `|v|` = `VEL_MAX_PX_PER_TICK` [PLACEHOLDER] on each axis, `n` = 360 and `n` = 2190, `p0` at plus and minus `WEB_COORD_BUDGET_PX` [PLACEHOLDER], negative velocities) WHEN each is run (a) uninterrupted and (b) restored at every tick from `t0 + n - 3` to `t0 + n + 3` THEN the pod pose after the arrival apply equals `p0 + v x n` exactly in every run. No random source is used. [D.1, D.2]
- **AC-WM-15a (auto)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* **Case A.** `t0 = 10000`, `n = 900`, `ARRIVE_SETTLE_TICKS` 45, an empty queue. GIVEN a snapshot taken after tick 10899 WHEN restored and run to tick 10901 THEN the pod slipped exactly `v x 1`, the pose after the arrival apply is `p1`, `EXPECTED_RESTORE_SLIP` is logged at DEBUG only (no WARN or higher), the restored run's persisted `arrive_T` is **10900** (nominal, equal to the uninterrupted run's); WHEN the run continues to tick 10957 THEN its `pod_settle` applies at **10945**, `first_boarded_T` is **10945**, the restored run's `web.state`, `body.state` and `layout.state` hashes equal the uninterrupted run's exactly at tick 10945 (no player intent in the window), **every other block equals it at tick 10957** (`T* + 12`) with **no accruals** (the three named exclusions of AC-WM-54 (b) are zero in Case A), and two restored runs from one snapshot produce identical `state_hash` sequences. [WM-13, E-23]
- **AC-WM-15b (auto)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* **Case B.** Same schedule. GIVEN a snapshot taken after tick 10944 (the pending `pod_settle` dropped) WHEN restored and run to tick 10946 THEN `pod_settle` applies at **10946** (lag 1), `first_boarded_T` is the nominal **10945**, the persisted `web.state`, `body.state` and `layout.state` hashes equal the uninterrupted run's exactly at the first tick `T*` where both are IDLE (10946), every other block equals it at tick 10958 (`T* + 12`) except the three bounded accruals, each at most its lag-1 bound (`ceil(1/6)` = 1 Graph step, 1 Suit run, `ceil(1/12)` = 1 Sanity accrual), asserted by the field-level comparator of AC-WM-54 (b). [WM-13, E-23]
- **AC-WM-16 (auto)** GIVEN a SYSTEM boundary command delayed by `a` = 4 ticks in the queue WHEN it applies THEN the observed offset `v x 3` is accepted without `LEG_POSE_MISMATCH`, and an observed offset of `v x 3 + (2, 0)` raises `LEG_POSE_MISMATCH` and is forced to `p1`. The same holds for `berth_step`. [WM-13, D.2, E-20]
- **AC-WM-17 (auto)** GIVEN two `pod_arrive` commands for one epoch WHEN both reach P2 THEN the second is `NOT_IN_LEG`, `transit_stale_cmds` increments and the kernel is not `FAULTED`; an older-epoch command is `STALE_EPOCH`. [E-08, E-09]
- **AC-WM-18 (auto)** GIVEN the hop at tick 10000 with `ARRIVE_SETTLE_TICKS` [PLACEHOLDER] 45 WHEN stepped THEN `transit_state` is ARRIVING from 10900, `pod_settle` is submitted at 10944 and applies at 10945, `destination_ready` is **false through tick 10944 and true from the `pod_settle` apply at 10945, before P5**, the avatar's `body_of_actor` is the target body at 10945, and the time in the pod is 945 ticks. [WM-14, tick table, C2]
- **AC-WM-19 (auto)** GIVEN a first hop to an UNSEEN derelict WHEN `pod_arrive` applies THEN `status` is SEEN and `unknown_seen` is true iff the lamp predicate of WM-18 holds at that tick (never from camera framing); WHEN `pod_settle` applies THEN `status` is BOARDED and `first_boarded_T` is set once; a later visit never regresses the status. [WM-14, WM-28]
- **AC-WM-20 (auto)** GIVEN the arrival cell blocked WHEN `pod_hop` is pressed THEN `ARRIVAL_BLOCKED` with no state change and no fuel. GIVEN a test hook at the Body double that forces `can_transfer` false inside `pod_settle` THEN the first failure starts exactly one reversal to the departure body (`leg_epoch + 1`, `leg_dir` flipped, 0 fuel, `ARRIVAL_RECHECK_FAILED` logged), a second failure places the avatar on the `arrive_*` cell with `ARRIVAL_FORCED` and the machine reaches IDLE, and the machine never stays in ARRIVING more than one extra tick. GIVEN a BACK leg whose home dock room is depressurised or breached THEN `pod_hop` is accepted (arrival safety is geometry-only), `ARRIVAL_BLOCKED` is not raised for a hazard, and the avatar arrives in the hazard room with the Graph's state unchanged by the hop (AC-WM-120 holds the Integration form) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. [WM-15, E-17, E-18, E-79]
- **AC-WM-10b (auto, table)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* Precedence table: infeasible pairs. For `pod_hop`, the pairs that cannot co-occur and are tested pairwise: (`TARGET_UNKNOWN`, `TARGET_ABSORBED`), (`TARGET_UNKNOWN`, `TARGET_HERE`), (`TARGET_UNKNOWN`, `TARGET_BERTHED`), (`TARGET_ABSORBED`, `TARGET_HERE`), (`TARGET_ABSORBED`, `TARGET_BERTHED`), (`TARGET_HERE`, `TARGET_BERTHED`), (`TARGET_BERTHED`, `BERTH_PENDING` payload form), and (`FUEL_LOW`, any state with `pod_at` a derelict, because `FUEL_LOW` is HOME-origin only). (`BERTH_PENDING` payload-less form, `TARGET_UNKNOWN`) CO-OCCUR (a payload-less press has no target) and the test asserts `BERTH_PENDING` first *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*. For `berth`: (`SOCKET_NOT_FREE`, `ALREADY_BERTHED`) (a weld clears the berth, and a berth never occupies the socket), (`NOT_DOCKED_HERE`, `ALREADY_BERTHED`) (no hop leaves HOME while a derelict is berthed, so the pod is at HOME) and (`NOT_BOARDED` for an unknown id, `NO_MATE_SOCKET` / `SOCKET_NOT_FREE`, which need a real target's `can_attach` preview). `NO_MATE_SOCKET` and `SOCKET_NOT_FREE` DO co-occur (both from one `can_attach` preview) and the test asserts `NO_MATE_SOCKET` first. The lone traveller's `BUSY` (IN_POD, read at the start of P2) versus `IN_TRANSIT` (machine not IDLE) is the same-tick versus later-tick case of WM-9, asserted at both ticks. [WM-9, WM-21, E-12, E-45, E-46]

### Group 7. Save/restore, events and death

- **AC-WM-54 (auto)** GIVEN snapshots at every tick within plus and minus 16 ticks of each phase boundary (departure, `pod_arrive`, `pod_settle`, each `berth_step`) and at every 16th tick elsewhere in one full sortie (about 2,000 ticks) WHEN each is restored and run to IDLE THEN (a) two restored runs from one snapshot are bit-identical, (b) **equality at the first IDLE tick** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* (**the three named bounded accruals below appear only in Case B, a snapshot that drops `pod_settle` itself, i.e. after tick `arrive_T + ARRIVE_SETTLE_TICKS - 1`; a snapshot that drops only `pod_arrive` (Case A) has none, AC-WM-15a; the accruals are asserted by a field-level comparator that masks the named fields (the target section's loaded clocks and wear age in `graph.state`, thermal load and hold counters in `suit.state`, level in `sanity.state`) and compares every other field exactly, not by a block hash, because a block hash cannot assert a bounded difference**): at the first tick `T*` at which both the restored and the uninterrupted run are IDLE (the tick numbers compared are equal and the command stream carries no player intent between the nominal boundary and `T*`), `state_hash` is **exactly equal for `web.state`, `body.state` and `layout.state`**, because every boundary value is keyed to a nominal tick (WM-13); **every other block is equal at `T* + 12`** (the largest hook cadence; the Controller's `IN_POD` exit one step late and Emitter re-arming are transient), **except three named bounded accruals**, numerically: (i) `graph.state`, the target section's loaded clocks, at most `ceil(lag / 6)` Graph steps (lag 1 on an empty queue: at most one step, i.e. the residual lamp battery stock higher by at most `LAMP_DJ_PER_STEP` 20 dJ and the section's wear age one step younger); (ii) `suit.state`, thermal load and hold counters, at most `ceil(lag / 6)` runs (the `IN_POD` band versus the target section's band; both run after the OQ-WM-41 ruling) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*; (iii) `sanity.state`, level, at most `ceil(lag / 12)` accruals of the `aboard` delta (`ABOARD_EXPOSURE`). The three exclusions close only if the owners adopt as-of accounting (ASK-G1b and its Suit and Sanity equivalents, OQ-WM-43), after which full equality at `T* + 12` is asserted; and (c) no clamp is applied (`clamp` counter 0). The exhaustive every-tick sweep (about 4 million ticks) is a nightly job, not a per-commit gate. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* **Test economics:** the per-commit job uses **boundary-seeded fixtures only**, one restore at each of the phase boundary tick minus 1, at it, plus 1 and plus `SLIP_MAX_TICKS`, for the departure, `pod_arrive`, `pod_settle` and the first and last `berth_step`, each run only to the first IDLE tick plus 12 (about 20 restores of at most 1,100 ticks each, a [PLACEHOLDER] budget of 60 s of CPU in `tests/integration/web_map_transit/`); the +/-16-tick neighbourhood sweep and the every-16th-tick sweep run **nightly** (about 2,000 restores, [PLACEHOLDER] budget 20 min), and the every-tick sweep weekly (about 4 million ticks, [PLACEHOLDER] budget 4 h); the same split applies to AC-WM-14, 23, 50, 51 and 126, whose over-100,000-tick simulations are nightly with a boundary-seeded per-commit subset. [WM-30, E-22 to E-28, QA F-1]
- **AC-WM-55 (auto)** GIVEN a content edit of `n` or a berth phase after a mid-leg or mid-berth save WHEN restored THEN the persisted resolved parameters are used and the arrival tick is unchanged; GIVEN `p1 != p0 + v x n` in the save THEN `WEB_RESTORE_LEG_INEXACT` and the kernel is not `RUNNING`. [WM-30, E-26]

### Group 12. Playtest and feel

*Protocol for AC-WM-81 and AC-WM-82: at least three outside testers is the floor, not the sample; think-aloud transcripts are coded by two raters against a written rubric ("unprompted" means before any facilitator mention of the exterior), disagreements resolved by a third. Results are reported as findings; the gate is that no item with a "none" bound has a hit.*

*Shared clause for AC-WM-81a to 81k: GIVEN the AC-WM-81 protocol session above, WHEN the transcripts are coded by two raters against the rubric, THEN each item below holds.*

- **AC-WM-81a (manual)** GIVEN the AC-WM-81 protocol session WHEN transcripts are coded THEN at least two of three testers mention the residual lamp or the exterior unprompted. [Player Fantasy Design Test summary]
- **AC-WM-81b (manual)** GIVEN the AC-WM-81 protocol session WHEN transcripts are coded THEN none describes the hop as a loading screen. [Design Test summary, guard rail 8]
- **AC-WM-81c (manual)** GIVEN the AC-WM-81 protocol session WHEN transcripts are coded THEN none asks for a skip before the fourth hop. [Design Test summary]
- **AC-WM-81d (auto witness check; manual note, non-gating)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* GIVEN a `pod_hop` from HOME at the defaults WHEN the departure applies THEN the HOME tank's stock read by the Graph falls by exactly `HOP_FUEL_ML` [PLACEHOLDER] on the same apply (the debit is visible to the Graph's witnesses and the reactor timer shortens by the corresponding burn time) and no Web Map & Transit element shows it. A 2 % debit of the tank is not something players can be expected to notice, so the playtest only **records** (no pass line) whether any tester mentions the fuel debit through the Graph's witnesses or the reactor timer. [Design Test summary]
- **AC-WM-81e (manual)** GIVEN the AC-WM-81 protocol session WHEN transcripts are coded THEN at least one uses the pod to escape a tell or breach, and none reports the pod refusing them during one (a `NOT_LIT` refusal at a pod console is a recorded defect: WM-8b makes it unreachable, AC-WM-128) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*. This is a telemetry-backed finding: the session includes a **scripted breach** on the derelict and the log shows each press result; the safety claim itself is gated by the automated AC-WM-61 and the lit-gate ACs, not by this playtest *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. [Design Test summary, WM-26]
- **AC-WM-81f (manual)** GIVEN the AC-WM-81 protocol session WHEN transcripts are coded THEN none reports a derelict refilling. [Design Test summary, WM-25]
- **AC-WM-81g (manual)** GIVEN the AC-WM-81 protocol session WHEN transcripts are coded THEN none reports berthing or hopping as a way to dodge the Web's attention for free (the berth, as the home-ship's own drive burn, is an Attention source; the hop is the escape verb). *(Route 1 ruled by the user, D59, OQ-WM-40, with the push made by Interaction; if the user later rules the berth silent, any hit is the recorded tripwire, not a defect of this system)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling G1)* [Design Test summary, WM-26, E-54] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-WM-81i (manual)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* GIVEN the AC-WM-81 protocol session WHEN transcripts and reviewer notes are coded THEN none uses the words *shaky*, *loading*, *floaty-fast*, *stutter*, *jolt*, *lurch* or *teleport* for the crossing or the berth, and the berth contact reads as a soft meeting and not as the Tell. [Feel Acceptance Criteria, AC-WM-79, AC-WM-80, AC-WM-115]
- **AC-WM-81j (manual)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* GIVEN the AC-WM-81 protocol session WHEN transcripts are coded THEN testers describe the berth as a closing that took time and cost something, heard and not seen, and none reports it as a teleport. [Feel Acceptance Criteria, AC-WM-117, AC-WM-117b]
- **AC-WM-81k (manual)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* GIVEN the AC-WM-81 protocol session run once with reduced motion on WHEN transcripts are coded THEN the crossing is reported as fully legible (scroll continues, drift stops, no flash) and no tester reports a missing or confusing beat. [Feel Acceptance Criteria, AC-WM-30]
- **AC-WM-138 (auto)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* Press-to-fade latency, this system's half. GIVEN a `pod_hop` press that reaches P2 at tick T WHEN the apply completes THEN `POD_DEPARTED` is emitted in the P5 of tick T (zero ticks of latency from this system) and `destination_ready` is false from that tick; the plate's fade starting within two frames of the press is the Pipeline's criterion (routed as a cross-GDD note; AC-WM-29 observes it in a launched build). [WM-10, WM-14, Feel Input Responsiveness]
- **AC-WM-139 (auto, Integration)** The Chart Table's R6 and R1b cases. GIVEN the pod at HOME, IDLE, derelict B berthed and unwelded, WHEN a payload-less `pod_hop` is submitted (the POD console press with no valid pick) THEN the reason is `BERTH_PENDING`, no state changes, no fuel moves; GIVEN nothing berthed and the same payload-less `pod_hop` THEN `TARGET_UNKNOWN`; GIVEN `pod_hop{target_id := B}` with B berthed THEN `TARGET_BERTHED`; GIVEN `pod_hop{target_id := A}` with B berthed THEN `BERTH_PENDING`; GIVEN the pod at A and a `berth` or `unberth` through a test double (no DOCK subject exists there) THEN `NOT_DOCKED_HERE`; GIVEN the pod at HOME, machine IDLE, in reach and lit THEN the DOCK subject is eligible, selects `unberth` iff `berthed_present()` and `berth` iff not, and the POD subject selects `pod_hop`. - **AC-WM-82a (manual)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* GIVEN the first leg WHEN observed THEN at least two of three testers describe the warm point as something they saw (rubric above). [D.6, WM-18]
- **AC-WM-82b (auto, scripted route)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review: the route is counted from AC-WM-49's script)* GIVEN a scripted 30-minute session (108,000 ticks) WHEN the summed `in_pod_ticks` (`POD_ARRIVED.tick - POD_DEPARTED.tick`) is computed THEN it is at most 6 % of session ticks for each of two scripts: the D.10 route of **four legs**, 4 x 945 = 3,780 ticks = **3.5 %**, and the AC-WM-49 route of **five legs** (home to A, A to home, home to B, B to home, home to A after the weld; the berth is not a leg), 5 x 945 = 4,725 ticks = **4.4 %** (the earlier text called it a six-leg route reading 5.25 %, which miscounted the legs). - **AC-WM-83 (auto)** GIVEN a debug override forcing each of the four hallucination classes on (with sanity at its lowest band) WHEN a hop is made THEN the refusal reason equals the validate result, the lamp point equals the WM-18 predicate, the docked-prop flag equals `pod_at`, and `web.view` is byte-identical to the unforced run. The HUD during the hold is not this system's and is not asserted. [Player Fantasy hallucination table, WM-35] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*

- **AC-WM-87 (auto)** UNSEEN is hoppable. GIVEN a new game WHEN `web.view` is read THEN A and B are listed with `status` UNSEEN and `unknown_seen` 0 and nothing else about them, and `pod_hop` to either is accepted with sufficient fuel. [E-47, OQ-WM-4]
- **AC-WM-88 (auto)** Console eligibility and pick. - **AC-WM-89 (auto)** BM7 limit and pose moments. GIVEN `set_body_pose` with `|coord|` = `WORLD_COORD_LIMIT` [PLACEHOLDER] and with that value plus 1 THEN it is accepted and rejected respectively; GIVEN a full route THEN pose-set calls occur only at `init_world`, the departure apply, `pod_arrive`, `pod_settle`, the `berth` / `unberth` applies and `berth_step` applies. - **AC-WM-90 (auto)** BM7 refused. - **AC-WM-91 (auto)** `unberth` reasons. - **AC-WM-93b (auto, Integration; provisional on ASK-PC-1)** The pod is not a safe room *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. GIVEN a seat that boarded with a scripted lethal status (hp at the DOWNED threshold, or the Suit stub's FAILED) WHEN the leg runs THEN the Controller enters STAGGERED, then DOWNED, then DEAD by its own rules with no pod exemption, DEAD triggers "restart from the last autosave" and the world block rolls back (`pod_at`, `transit_state` and the fuel the hop spent together, AC-WM-58). - **AC-WM-94 (auto)** HOME is never dormant. - **AC-WM-95 (auto)** `destination_ready` over time. GIVEN a hop and a berth WHEN sampled each tick THEN it is false at every tick of LEG, ARRIVING and BERTHING, and true in IDLE; it is true at init. [WM-14, WM-36]
- **AC-WM-96 (auto)** Late projector. - **AC-WM-103 (auto)** Events. - **AC-WM-105 (auto)** The tell survives a hop and the void applies. GIVEN a TELL on derelict A (a non-home locus) whose only recorded occupant is the seat WHEN the seat hops home and the fixture forces `breach_due_T` to fall inside the leg (hop tick + 300, inside the 900-tick leg) THEN `tell_epoch`, `tell_len` and `breach_due_T` are unchanged by the hop (the clock runs; `tell_remaining` falls by one per tick, so the earlier wording '`tell_remaining` is unchanged' is withdrawn), no `LOCUS_FALLBACK` is recorded at any run between the hop and the due run, `locus_section_id` stays pinned to A, and at the first run with T >= `breach_due_T` the phase is CALM, `tell_outcome` ABORTED, STRESS deposited on the home body is 0 and no beacon command is submitted (AT-22, AC-AT-46 clause 1). GIVEN the same TELL with the fixture forcing `breach_due_T` after the seat has hopped home and back and settled (hop home at t_h, settle t_h + 945, return hop the next tick, settle t_h + 1891; due run at t_h + 2000; a fixture override, since at the defaults a natural due run cannot fall after a round trip, E-60) THEN `locus_vacated` is false at the due run and the breach proceeds on A with 0 STRESS on the home ship. GIVEN a TELL whose locus is the home ship and the seat hops to A THEN the tell continues and the due run breaches on the home ship (not voidable; nobody aboard is Attention E-73). `HOP_LOUD_ON_DEPARTURE` is false by default and the hop `push_stimulus` count stays 0 (a berth in the same session adds exactly 1 at its apply, pushed by Interaction and counted by AC-WM-26b) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling G1)* *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*. [WM-26, E-52, E-60]

- **AC-WM-107 (lint)** Determinism hygiene. - **AC-WM-122 (auto, Integration, ACTIVE)** Home fault while in the pod: no deferral. - **AC-WM-125 (lint, BLOCKING CI)** Grid test and corner sweep *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. **(a) Knobs tied by inequalities are tested on a grid with an expected verdict per tuple, not by corners.** GIVEN the grid `LAMP_VIEW_MIN_TICKS` in {60, 90, 112, 136} x `LEG_STANDOFF_PX` in {24, 48, 70, 96} x `abs(v)` in {1, 2} x field `F_x` in {160, 192} x cull {off, on}, WHEN D.6 is evaluated THEN the lint verdict equals the oracle `LAMP_VIEW_MIN_TICKS <= floor((F_x - LEG_STANDOFF_PX) / abs(v))` for every tuple (circle test when the cull is on), with these fixed expectations at `F_x` 160, cull off: (90, 48, 1) PASS (112), (90, 48, 2) FAIL (56), (90, 96, 1) FAIL (64), (60, 96, 1) PASS (64), (60, 96, 2) FAIL (32), (136, 24, 1) PASS (136), (136, 48, 1) FAIL (112); a leg whose velocity points away from the lamp (`sign(v_axis) != sign(s_axis)`) FAILS in every tuple (the direction conjunct); and a fixture with `F_axis < abs(s_axis)` is rejected (`view_ticks` 0) instead of passing the squared test. GIVEN the grid `MIN_FRAME_RATE` in {1, 24, 30, 60} x `ARRIVE_MARGIN_TICKS` in {10, 20, 60} x `ARRIVE_SETTLE_TICKS` in {16, 17, 24, 45, 180} WHEN D.13 and L3b are evaluated THEN the verdict equals `ARRIVE_SETTLE_TICKS >= ceil(331 / BUILD_TILES_PER_FRAME) x ceil(TICK_RATE / MIN_FRAME_RATE) + ARRIVE_MARGIN_TICKS` and `ARRIVE_SETTLE_TICKS >= SLIP_MAX_TICKS + 2` for every tuple (for example, as (`MIN_FRAME_RATE`, `ARRIVE_MARGIN_TICKS`, `ARRIVE_SETTLE_TICKS`), (30, 20, 24) PASS, (24, 20, 24) FAIL at 26, (30, 10, 16) FAIL on L3b only (14 <= 16 passes D.13), (1, 10, 180) PASS at `2 x 60 + 10`); `MIN_FRAME_RATE` below 1 and `BUILD_TILES_PER_FRAME` below 1 are rejected, not divided by. **(b) Independent, monotone knobs keep the corner sweep:** GIVEN the stated boxes of `LEG_LATERAL_PX`, `SLOT_PITCH_PX`, `WEB_COORD_BUDGET_PX`, `EXTERIOR_VISIBLE_RADIUS_PX` and `MAX_WORLD_RECORDS` WHEN every corner is evaluated against D.14 (a), (b) and (c) THEN each corner either passes or is listed as a failing corner in the report, and no stated range contains an unreachable value. [D.6, D.13, D.14] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*

GIVEN the content tree and Emitter State's registry values (`DOCK_STATUS_BASE_PM`, `DOCK_STATUS_REACH_PX`, `LIT_ON_PM`, `LIT_OFF_PM`) read at lint time, never literals, WHEN linted THEN every dock room (airlock, `a_dock`, `b_dock`) places exactly one DOCK_STATUS glow on its docked-podcraft prop; for every CONSOLE anchor in that room (POD in all three, DOCK in the airlock) G1 holds: `c(d_c) >= LIT_ON_PM` + Emitter's I-DOCK margin with `d_c <= DOCK_STATUS_CONSOLE_MAX_PX` (24); and for the arrival cell G2 holds: `c(d_arr) < LIT_OFF_PM`, i.e. `d_arr >= DOCK_STATUS_ARR_MIN_PX`; both computed with Emitter's integer `F` and `c` read from the registry at lint time, never literals; a layout whose arrival cell is lit by the glow fails the build (Authoring lint `DOCK_GLOW_ARRIVAL_DARK`). Boundary cases (recomputed): `d_arr` 36 gives `F` = floor(12000 / 48) = 250, `c` = 125 >= 120, FAIL; `d_arr` 37 gives `F` = 229, `c` = 114, PASS; `d_c` 24 gives `c` = 250 >= 150, PASS; `d_c` 25 gives `F` = floor(23000 / 48) = 479, `c` = 239, still lit but violates `d_c` <= 24, FAIL; `DOCK_STATUS_CONSOLE_MAX_PX` 49 with reach 48: Emitter's I-DOCK refuses at boot. *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)* [E-16, WM-8b, D.15, OQ-WM-39, OQ-WM-48]

### Group 14. Presentation criteria added by the art-director consult

- **AC-WM-110 (manual, Visual)** GIVEN the leg's first frame (A and B, OUT and BACK) WHEN captured THEN one Tungsten pool is on `#000000`, no other luminous element is present, and filaments appear only inside the pool mask; the final approach frame shows the lamp offset clear of the pod silhouette by at least 8 px and inside the pool radius. [VA-R1, VA-R2, CF-AD-5]
- **AC-WM-111 (manual, Visual)** GIVEN a lit-lamp frame (node OK, battery above 0) and a dark-lamp frame (battery 0, or node destroyed with a charged battery) WHEN captured THEN the lit frame shows a 4x4 Tungsten core with halo at `exterior_anchor_px` and is the highest local contrast in frame; the dark frame shows no core and no halo and the 8x8 housing visible in the pool on the final frames. [VA-R9, WM-18]
- **AC-WM-112 (manual, Visual)** GIVEN the hold WHEN captured THEN the plate is flat `#000000`, the HUD is present, and there is no text, spinner, ring or percentage. [VA-R1, PIPE:264]
- **AC-WM-113 (auto, Visual gate)** Palette scan. GIVEN every exterior, hold and chart capture WHEN scanned THEN no pixel lies in the violet band (hue 270 to 330) or the flesh band (hue 5 to 30, S 20 to 60, V 45 to 85), and warm-lit Hull Grey and the bronze iridescence stop under the pool have hue at least 36 degrees (or saturation under 20 % in-pool). [ART 4.4, section 4 of the Visual/Audio Requirements] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-WM-114 (auto)** Restore mid-LEG composition. GIVEN a restore at any tick of an OUT leg and of a BACK leg THEN the filament layout, the target's lamp state and (on a BACK leg) the astern departure derelict with its lamp at the restore tick equal those of an uninterrupted leg at that tick (all are pure functions of persisted data, including `origin_id`, WM-30) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*, and the kernel `LOADING` hold precedes the cut. [VA, WM-30]
- **AC-WM-115 (manual, Visual)** GIVEN a berth in a launched build WHEN the contact beat is captured as a frame sequence THEN the camera offset peaks at `BERTH_SETTLE_PX` [PLACEHOLDER] 2 art px, returns to 0 within 400 ms, the HUD pixels do not move, no exterior is visible through any port, and the release Settle on `unberth` peaks at half of that. Provisional on the Pipeline accepting the camera impulse (ASK-AD-6); if refused, the contact beat is audio plus the weld prompt only and this criterion is waived. [VA-R4, M8]
- **AC-WM-117 (auto)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* The berth bed is wired. GIVEN a berth WHEN the audio event log is read THEN the berth bed request is issued at the `berth` apply tick, keyed on the `transit_state == BERTHING` state edge only (no `t0` or `n`, AC-AA-128), ends at the `BERTHED` contact cue, and the Audio source passes the L-10 tag lint (the lint, not a spectral test, is the MVP check that the bed is not the Tell). [WM-21, VA-R4, AA-33 (g)]
- **AC-WM-117b (manual, Visual/Audio)** The berth is witnessed, not asserted. GIVEN a berth in a launched build WHEN played from the `berth` press to the weld prompt THEN a sustained drive-hum and hull-strain bed is audible from the apply tick to the contact cue (spanning BERTHING, `BERTH_TICKS` [PLACEHOLDER]), it is non-positional and not keyed to any progress datum, it is not mistakable for the Tell (reviewed against the Tell cue), and the contact Settle and weld prompt follow it; under reduced motion the bed, the cue and the prompt remain and only the Settle is removed. The audio assets and triggers are the audio-director's. Designed: the berth bed is Audio AA-33 (g), read from `web.view.transit_state == BERTHING` (state edge only, no `t0` / `n`), discharged by AC-AA-128. [WM-21, VA-R4, Visual/Audio section 8, Game Feel] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-WM-118 (manual, Visual)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* The refusal thresholds are world marks. GIVEN the home FUEL_TANK in a launched build WHEN captured at the default zoom THEN two painted reserve marks are present, the pod mark at 9.5 % and the berth mark at 11.5 % of the tank's height (at the defaults) and none at the floor itself (7.5 %), each static (no animation, no emission, no number), not Alarm Red, violet or flesh, and unchanged between a full tank and a tank below the marks; the sight-glass beside them is a separate, lie-capable reading. *(provisional — ASK-AD-9, the art, is open; waived, with AC-WM-81h as the tripwire, if refused)* [WM-20, ASK-AD-9]
- **AC-WM-119 (lint, BLOCKING CI)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* `FLOOR_MARK_MATCHES_KNOB`. GIVEN each authored reserve-mark height fraction, `HOP_FUEL_FLOOR_ML` [PLACEHOLDER] and the verb's cost over the tank capacity WHEN checked at content load THEN the pod mark equals (floor + `HOP_FUEL_ML`) / capacity and the berth mark equals (floor + `BERTH_FUEL_ML`) / capacity to within one art pixel, no mark is authored at the floor itself, else the content build fails. [WM-20, D.5]

### Gaps / untestable as written

- **G-1.** Closed 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling R12: AC-WM-77's "minimum-spec target" is RM-DESKTOP (Windows 10/11, 6-core i5-9400F / Ryzen 5 2600 class, 8 GB, GTX 1050 Ti / RX 570 class, 1080p60, P99 ceiling 16.6 ms) and RM-DECK (Steam Deck LCD, 1280x800, 30 fps target, P99 ceiling 33.3 ms), built as a release export (`forward_plus`, vsync on, 120-frame warm-up); the measurement is pending the spike on both. *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling R12)*
- **G-2.** AC-WM-67 L8 `LEG_FOUL` (the pod's path must not cross a wreck hull) asserts a rule without a geometry: the GDD states "the rule, not the geometry". It needs a named content tool and a numeric clearance; until then it is checked by review of the screenshots of AC-WM-110.
- **G-3.** *(closed 2026-10-27 — re-review)* AC-WM-39: the reason for a `berth` target that is not a BOARDED DERELICT is `NOT_BOARDED` and `NO_MATE_SOCKET` precedes `SOCKET_NOT_FREE` (WM-9; OQ-WM-19 closed); the infeasible pairs are tabulated in AC-WM-10b.
- **G-4.** AC-WM-74 is arithmetic only; the measured loaded-tile count at S4 against `TILES_WORLD_MVP` 600 needs a real Layout build.
- **G-5.** AC-WM-81 and AC-WM-82 sample three testers: they are findings that gate on "none" hits, not statistics.
- **G-6.** AC-WM-37 gate (2) has no value until the first playtest measures the typical home draw ("typical draw" is undefined before then); gate (3)'s threshold must be agreed before the run.
- **G-7.** Every criterion marked *(provisional — X)* cannot pass until the owning neighbour GDD exists; the register of such dependencies is the ask register in Interactions with Other Systems.
- **G-8.** AC-WM-115 and the contact Settle depend on the Pipeline accepting a world-camera impulse not currently in its camera states (ASK-AD-6); if it is refused the criterion is waived, not failed.
- **G-9.** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* The review's list of twelve untestable ACs was not supplied. The untestable halves found on re-reading were converted: AC-WM-26 (code review, now lint `NO_PUSH_STIMULUS`, re-review), AC-WM-36 (manual half, now lint `NO_WM_FUEL_STORE`), AC-WM-53 (records the Graph's answer, now a keyed fixture), AC-WM-77 (wall clock, now `tests/performance`), AC-WM-117 (split into auto wiring and manual quality), AC-WM-37 (gate 1 now a lint, gate 3 now a first-`FUEL_LOW` sortie index). The remaining judgment criteria are the manual Visual/Feel rows and the playtest findings.
- **G-10.** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* AC-WM-133 and 134 cannot pass until the decisions or asks they name are answered (ASK-MSA-1); AC-WM-127a to c and 128 are active after the OQ-WM-39 ruling; Emitter State's class and E10 have landed and Authoring's placement and lints are specified in DA-46 (2026-10-28, G1); they run when the templates exist *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*

## Open Questions

*Owner: **user** (a decision only the user can make), **agent** (another GDD's author or a specialist must answer), **GDD** (this document, next revision). Target: **playtest** (the kill/continue playtest, CON:483-485), **next GDD** (the named neighbour), **ADR** (an architecture decision record at the architecture phase). The pending user decisions that used to come first (OQ-WM-39, OQ-WM-40, OQ-WM-41) were all ruled by the user on 2026-10-27, and every default the GDD followed pending confirmation is confirmed 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling R20; the rows owned by **user** that remain are playtest and tuning questions, each with a default the GDD already follows, so nothing here blocks authoring. *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling R20)* Ids OQ-WM-1 to OQ-WM-11 were reserved by Detailed Design; ruled ones are listed under the table.*
